(2) /save accepts any cross-origin POST: curl -X POST -H 'Content-Type: text/plain' -H 'Origin: http://evil.example' -d '{"picked":["v1"]}' /save -> ok:true, so any web page open in the human's browser can rewrite picks.json (Host is 127.0.0.1:P, passes). - 473 | 1795310425 | kit-r2 | task:UAK-130 | reject | UAK-130 | Rejected SHA d68b644 by kit-r2 · 1) Read-your-writes broken by a race: rc_put only compares the reader's NOW (its start second) with the writer's stamp (also its start second), so a status that fetched before a claim pushed but started in the same second or after the claimer started overwrites the claimer's refresh with the old state. Repro (shared clone, 2 agents, UAK_READ_CACHE=on, git shim holding the reader after its fetch): agent-1 status at T0+100 held after fetch; agent-2 claims HACK-002 at T0+100 (rc 0); release reader; agent-2 status at T0+101 shows AVAILABLE=3 CLAIMED=0 'claims as of 1 s ago'; agent-2 next at T0+102 offers HACK-001 AVAILABLE 'wt new HACK-001 --agent agent-2' although it holds HACK-002. Expected: the claim is shown (stamp must be ordered by the clone tip or the write must win), got: stale cache newer than the write. 2) rc_get/rc_put read 'current' with '< file 2>/dev/null' (redirect order), so bash prints 'cache.sh: line 228/253: .../uak-read-cache/current: No such file or directory' on stderr on the first read in a clone and on every read after any write that drops the cache (failed claim, done, merge). 3) a cache entry whose tx is empty or has no .git makes status fail with GIT_ERROR exit 1 instead of falling back to a fetch. 4) scope: only status and next use the cache; inbox/board/timeline/metrics are neither cached nor accept --fresh, against the acceptance. Fine: claim of a stale-visible task still refused, stale reads say their age, --json claims_age, Read-Cache 0/abc/-5/huge/CRLF, backwards/forward clock, snapshot identical, all suites and lint green; real repo status 2.9 s -> 1.1 s, 0 fetches.. Fix, push, and rerun /uak-ship
- 474 | 1795310841 | kit-r1 | task:UAK-131 | reject | UAK-131 | Rejected SHA fd5774b by kit-r1 · Repro (fixture, test_merge_engine_policy helpers, Merge-Engine: ci on base, approved by another identity): check-runs list EMPTY + commit status 'success/1' (any collaborator can POST a status to their own tip) merges an engine file; a single check 'x/completed/success' (or an empty-named one) also merges. rm_ci_checks counts the checks that exist, not the required ones: nothing ties green to the base's required_status_checks, and the gate pushes to base itself so branch protection never enforces them; doctor only warns. A diff may also change .github/workflows or .uak/bin/smoke (engine subset, in Paths) and so neuter the checks/smoke that judge it. Fix: with ci require every required context of the base (read from the forge) to be a success check run, none configured/unreadable = human; do not count bare commit statuses; send workflow/smoke changes to the human or require checks from the base's workflows. Required behaviour was 143/130 (or no line); a killed command now counts as a success in 'errors 0 of N'. Lesser: a script whose own EXIT trap calls 'exit N' (repro: trap 'echo x; exit 6' EXIT) writes NO line because ledger_exit is chained after it; autopsy, design and design-gallery (wrappers that exec python) still write exit/ms '-' (disclosed) so they keep 'outcome not recorded'.
